### Coach, guided topic conversations and mission role-play (where an agent would plug in). All paths below are relative to repo root "C:\Users\Nima\Desktop\MINE\My_projects\Dutch Learning Platform\Final_Project_v1" (API code under apps/api/src/dlp).

- **partly** — coaching/service.py candidate_plan is pure deterministic code returning exactly 3 items; the only model involvement is an optional reorder (_rank_once, complete_once) validated by validate_ranking; plans are cached by history version.
  - Correction: The core claim holds, with these corrections. (a) candidate_plan returns a tuple (plan, evidence), not just a plan. Three items are guaranteed only because TopicBank has min_length=100 topics and there are 4 SKILLS. (b) Only POST /coach/plan writes the cache. GET never writes and returns the uncached deterministic plan until some POST has happened. (c) A POST with an already-used request_id returns the stored response no matter what the history is now (:262-267). (d) Fallback plans are cached too, including fixture mode, Azure without paid_usage_enabled, provider outage and usage limit (:278-285). After one transient failure, further POSTs for the same history_version return the cached 'fallback' and never retry the model. (e) The model is skipped completely when can_personalise is False, i.e. when there is no current history.
  - Evidence: candidate_plan (domains/coaching/service.py:160-221) has no I/O. It builds items with add() and the skill loop stops at `if len(items) == 3: break` (:208-209). Test test_fresh_plan_has_three_real_localized_activities_without_claiming_weakness asserts 3 unique ids. The only model call is _rank_once -> providers.chat.complete_once(..., schema=PlanRanking, max_output_tokens=600, temperature=0.0, prompt_version=POLICY_VERSION) (:311), checked by validate_ranking (:235-243). Cache: get_plan (:224-232) and personalise_plan (:271-276) look up CoachPlanRequest by (learner_id, stage_id, history_version, policy_version). Only personalise_plan inserts a row (:282-285).
- **partly** — Mission role-play (practice/workflow.py) runs propose_action (model) → apply_action (code) → compose_reply (model, anchored on a fixed line, with a contradiction guard that falls back to the fixed line); service scenarios use fixed lines only.
  - Correction: (a) The mission workflow uses the retrying chat.complete (max_attempts = settings.chat_max_attempts), not complete_once. (b) The guard returns `anchor or reply`, so when the phase has no fixed line (anchor == '') a contradicting model reply is still shown. (c) Service scenarios still make the model call propose_action; only the reply is fixed. The reply is the fixed line, optionally prefixed by the 'repeat'/'clarify' fixed lines, or the hard-coded 'Het gesprek is geannuleerd. Er is niets afgesproken.' Exactly one model call per service turn (test_service_missions.py:132-141 asserts len(chat.calls) == 1). (d) Generative compose_reply (temperature 0.3, CHARACTER_REPLY_VERSION) therefore runs only in appointment-change. (e) If the first call fails, TurnFailed raises a 502, the failed turn row is kept and both reservations are released (turns.py:177-184).
  - Evidence: workflow.py:58-131: the graph is propose_action -> validate_action (apply_action) -> compose_reply. Service branch: :89-99 returns reply_source 'fixed_line'. Guard: :119-120 `if not reply or _contradicts_decision(...): return {"reply_nl": anchor or reply, ...}`. On ProviderError: :112-114 `anchor or "Sorry, kunt u dat herhalen?"`. Both calls use chat.complete (:65, :109), not complete_once. Only content/missions/appointment-change is kind 'appointment'. course-message, lunch-order and shop-return have `"kind": "service"`.
- **partly** — Writing corrections (curriculum/writing.py) are validated span-by-span, any invalid span rejects the whole reply, and nothing is persisted.
  - Correction: No correction is persisted, but usage counters are. model_calls=1 and tokens=result.total_tokens are committed before validation (writing.py:147-148), so a rejected reply is still charged (test_unusable_paid_output_is_counted_but_never_applied). A provider exception releases both reservations (:143-146). In fixture mode the function returns review_status 'fixture_unreviewed' with no call. It uses chat_strong.complete (retrying), not complete_once.
  - Evidence: validate_corrections (writing.py:45-80) raises ProviderError for any correction that is not a unique exact match, is blank, has original == replacement, adds more than 5 words, cuts a word in part, or overlaps another. The route maps this to 503 'Writing feedback is temporarily unavailable. Your draft has not been changed.' (routes_curriculum.py:167). No model or table write exists in writing_feedback. test_writing_feedback_requires_auth_and_never_mutates_practice_or_exam asserts CurriculumPractice is None and attempt.submission == {}.
- **partly** — Mission prompts hard-code level A2 and all four missions are A2.
  - Correction: A2 is hard-coded only in the two appointment-kind prompts. The service-kind interpretation prompt names no level, and service replies are fixed lines. mission.cefr_target is never passed into any prompt. All four missions are indeed A2.
  - Evidence: practice/prompts.py:103 'Je analyseert wat een taalleerder (niveau A2) zegt...' (appointment propose prompt). :135 '...Belgisch Standaardnederlands op niveau A2: korte zinnen...' (character reply). The service propose prompt :86 says 'beginnende taalleerder' with no level. content/missions/{appointment-change,course-message,lunch-order,shop-return}/mission.json:5 all have `"cefr_target": "A2"`.

### background jobs, transactions, error model and API contract

- **partly** — Three near-identical per-router _run exception mappers exist (routes_stories, routes_videos, routes_curriculum). Domain errors disagree on .reason vs .detail. ProviderError maps to 502 in some routers and 503 in others.
  - Correction: There are three definitions, but they are not near-identical and they cover about 8 routers. Differences: curriculum catches CurriculumError only (not VocabError), plus AudioError, plus OperationalError sqlstate 55P03 -> 409 (re-raises anything else), and takes a `provider_detail` kwarg. stories catches CurriculumError, VocabError and AudioError. videos catches CurriculumError and VocabError, with no AudioError and no OperationalError. The 502/503 split is by route family: practice, speech and missions explicitly map ProviderUnavailable->503 and ProviderError->502 via HTTPException. All _run users map every ProviderError (including Unavailable) to 503 via JSONResponse. UsageLimitExceeded has neither .reason nor .detail; it carries metric, scope, requested and remaining. It becomes `HTTPException(429, detail=str(exc))` (which rolls back) in practice, speech and missions, and `JSONResponse(429, {"detail": "Your practice allowance is used up. Try again later."})` (which commits) in _run. Practice speech_turn instead uses `_speech_error` JSONResponse. routes_library.py has its own inline CurriculumError->HTTPException mapping.
  - Evidence: routes_curriculum.py:54-72, routes_stories.py:37-48 and routes_videos.py:29-38 define _run. curriculum._run is imported and reused by routes_topics.py:8, routes_topic_conversations.py:12, routes_coach.py:5 and routes_content_review.py:6. Errors using .detail: CurriculumError (curriculum/service.py:41-45) and its subclasses StoryError and VideoError, plus VocabError (stories/vocab.py:26-30). Errors using .reason: PracticeError (practice/service.py:16-20), AudioError (speech/audio.py:25-29, status_code keyword-only), AssertionError_ (identity/assertions.py:25-30). The 502 mappings are routes_practice.py:161 (TurnFailed), :282, :423; routes_speech.py:42, :90; routes_missions.py:139. The 503 mappings are every _run (curriculum :64-68, stories :46-48, videos :36-38) and ProviderUnavailable in the explicit handlers.
- **partly** — Observability: plain-text logs, uvicorn access log disabled, one histogram, 10% sampled spans, no trace propagation from the web proxy.
  - Correction: The access log is disabled only in the production image. Locally, `scripts/run.py` api_command (:190-192) starts uvicorn with access logs on. Telemetry is a no-op unless APPLICATIONINSIGHTS_CONNECTION_STRING is set (observability.py:14). `configure_azure_monitor(logger_name="dlp.telemetry", ...)` exports only logs from the `dlp.telemetry` logger, which nothing uses, so application logs never reach App Insights. The request middleware itself logs nothing. Background jobs produce no spans or metrics. Unhandled errors in production log only the type name and request_id (main.py:103-105).
  - Evidence: main.py:43-44 `logging.basicConfig(... format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(name)s %(message)s")`. observability.py:30 creates a single `create_histogram("http.server.request.duration", unit="s")`. :26 `sampling_ratio=0.1`. The web proxy (apps/web/app/api/[...path]/route.ts:19) forwards only content-type, accept, x-requested-with, accept-language and range, plus x-request-id and authorization (:103-104), with no traceparent. request_span (observability.py:33-41) starts a root SERVER span without extracting any context. Dockerfile:32 passes `--no-access-log`.
- **partly** — The read-aloud upload route reads the whole body unbounded while other speech routes bound the read.
  - Correction: The difference at the API is real, but its effect is limited. The only ingress is the web proxy, which caps bodies at `MAX_BODY_BYTES = 6 * 1024 * 1024` (route.ts:35, 106-111), though it buffers the whole body before checking. Starlette has already spooled the multipart file to a SpooledTemporaryFile before any handler runs. The bounded read in the other routes limits only the in-memory copy, not what the API receives. The read-aloud route also reads the upload after taking the episode FOR UPDATE lock (routes_stories.py:174-180).
  - Evidence: routes_stories.py:180 `upload_bytes=audio.file.read()` has no limit. routes_speech.py:29 and routes_practice.py:265 use `audio.file.read(settings.max_upload_bytes + 1)`. transcribe_upload then rejects anything over max_upload_bytes (5 MiB) with AudioError 413 (speech/service.py:41-42).

### web data layer, component structure and test setup (apps/web)

- **partly** — Multiple-choice UIs are implemented separately in at least four places (CurriculumControls.tsx, QuestionList.tsx, EpisodeReader.tsx, VideoPlayer.tsx); recorders are implemented in at least three ways (hold-to-talk in SpeakingStep/SpeechCheck via lib/client/recorder.ts; click toggle in CurriculumControls; toggle in EpisodeReader).
  - Correction: The multiple-choice part is right, and there are at least five (add AlphabetPractice). The recorder part is misleading: there is one recorder engine, useRecorder in lib/client/recorder.ts, used by all three. The UI wrappers and upload targets differ: (1) hold-to-talk via talkButtonHandlers, posting to practice/sessions/{id}/turns/speech (SpeakingStep) or speech/transcribe (SpeechCheck); (2) the CurriculumRecorder click toggle with a word-bound check, posting to speech/transcribe with keep_recording=true, reused by CurriculumLesson, CurriculumTest, SkillTopicPractice and TopicConversation; (3) the ReadAloud click toggle, posting to stories/episodes/{id}/read-aloud. EpisodeReader and VideoPlayer also duplicate Glossary (EpisodeReader.tsx:128-145, VideoPlayer.tsx:122-139) and the friendly() error mapper (:13-20 in both).
  - Evidence: Multiple choice: CurriculumControls.tsx:12-19 CurriculumQuestions (controlled, judged locally against answer_index); QuestionList.tsx:26-129 (each answer judged by the server through an onAnswer promise, plus HelpLadder); EpisodeReader.tsx:147-168 Questions and VideoPlayer.tsx:141-162 Questions (near-identical: one POST of all answers with a stable newRequestId, plain-string options); plus a fifth, AlphabetPractice.tsx:31 (button options). Recorders: all three UIs call the same hook useRecorder from lib/client/recorder.ts:45. CurriculumControls.tsx:9,143 (CurriculumRecorder: onClick toggle, uploads to speech/transcribe at :134); EpisodeReader.tsx:10,107-119 (ReadAloud: onClick toggle, uploads through stories.readAloud); SpeakingStep.tsx:12,179,339 and SpeechCheck.tsx:8,61,114 (hold-to-talk through {...talkButtonHandlers(() => void recorder.start(), recorder.stop)}).
- **partly** — WritingStep.tsx passes a hard-coded stageId "a2" to WritingCoach; 'De Lindestraat' is hard-coded in TodayHome/EpisodeReader although the API returns the series title.
  - Correction: The "a2" part is right, though it currently matches every mission, and cefr_target is upper-case "A2", so it would need lower-casing. The Lindestraat part needs two corrections. TodayHome already has plan.series.title (used at :71 and :148), but EpisodeCard (exported, also used by StoryLibrary.tsx:38) only receives an EpisodeSummary. EpisodeReader cannot get the title from its API response without an API change or an extra fetch. The cover span has className "cover-town" but shows the series title, while the API also returns a separate `town` (the e2e fixture uses "Zilverdonk").
  - Evidence: WritingStep.tsx:231 `<WritingCoach stageId="a2" text={text} disabled={busy} onApply={...}/>`; WritingCoach posts to `curriculum/${stageId}/writing-feedback` (WritingCoach.tsx:25). All four missions have "cefr_target": "A2" (content/missions/*/mission.json:5), and LessonShell has mission.cefr_target (LessonShell.tsx:263) but does not pass it to WritingStep. Hard-coded strings: TodayHome.tsx:51 headline "Hoe gaat het verder in de Lindestraat?", TodayHome.tsx:165 (EpisodeCard cover span), EpisodeReader.tsx:38 (cover span). The API's series_view (apps/api/src/dlp/domains/stories/service.py:81-86) returns title and town in GET today (:377) and GET stories (:386). episode_view (service.py:337-357), used by GET stories/episodes/{id}, has no series title. Every series gets title=SERIES_TITLE (service.py:64; bible.py:11 SERIES_TITLE = "De Lindestraat").
- **partly** — The project lint setup forbids state-setting inside effect bodies (docs/ENGINEERING.md) which is why components use a "result keyed by params" pattern.
  - Correction: The convention is documented, but I could not confirm that a rule enforces it. eslint.config.mjs does not name react-hooks/set-state-in-effect. Any enforcement would come from eslint-plugin-react-hooks v7's preset through eslint-config-next, and node_modules is absent, so I could not inspect it. Run `npm run lint` before relying on it. The pattern is also not universal: TodayHome, VideoLibrary, EpisodeReader, WordReview, StoryLibrary, CurriculumPath and CurriculumProgress simply call setState in promise callbacks. The "which is why" link is plausible but inferred.
  - Evidence: docs/ENGINEERING.md:98 "TypeScript strict; ESLint with the Next.js rules; no state-setting inside effect bodies." eslint.config.mjs:1-12 only spreads eslint-config-next/core-web-vitals and eslint-config-next/typescript, with no explicit rules. package-lock.json:3036-3049 locks eslint-config-next 16.3.5, which depends on eslint-plugin-react-hooks ^7.0.0 (lock: 7.1.1). The only eslint-disable is TopicConversation.tsx:117 (react-hooks/exhaustive-deps). The keyed-result pattern appears in ContentReviewQueue.tsx:39-49 (`loaded: {key, data}`, loadKey = `${stage}-${offset}-${revision}`), LearningLibrary.tsx:57-60 and :86-88 (key = JSON.stringify([...])) and :43 (overview?.stage_id === stageId), SkillTopicPractice.tsx:28-34,63, and WritingCoach.tsx:30 (`valid = feedback?.original_text === text && feedback.stage_id === stageId`). LearningLibrary.tsx:63-64 and :98-99 also defer `setResult(null); setError(false)` into a setTimeout callback.

### Factual errors the agent-architecture judge found in the design proposals

- own-loop A15 and agent-framework A15: test_release_refuses_broken_conversations_before_database_connection lives in apps/api/tests/test_learning_support_release.py (line 33), not tests/test_release.py.
- langgraph P5-09 and agent-framework A03 say tests/test_turns.py::test_a_model_failure_keeps_the_failed_turn_and_releases_usage must change. It patches dlp.domains.practice.workflow.build_graph with an object whose invoke raises RuntimeError before any provider call (test_turns.py:190-226), so 'nothing sent, used 0' stays correct; own-loop is right that it stays unchanged. The only pinned test that must flip is tests/test_writing_feedback.py::test_model_outage_releases_reservations (plain ProviderError, expects two releases).
- langgraph P5-09 and agent-framework A03: a uniform 'ProviderError -> 502' mapper contradicts tests/test_topic_conversations.py::test_uncertain_outage_retains_draft_turn_and_conservative_allowance, which pins 503 for a plain ProviderError through routes_curriculum._run; neither lists it for update. The final design leaves router status codes alone (P8).
- own-loop A01: 'one agent_runs row per logical call' while tests/test_writing_feedback.py stays green is not possible as written: those tests call writing_feedback(None, ...) with session=None, stubbed usage.reserve/commit/release (positional signatures, asserted event order) and a SimpleNamespace provider exposing only .complete. A gateway that inserts through the session or opens a savepoint raises. The same hazard applies to own-loop A09 persisting writing_reviews inside writing_feedback().
- own-loop A02 (AST guard 'only domains/llm and providers call chat methods') and langgraph ('LlmGateway is the only caller of the port') contradict pinned tests that call topic_conversations.workflow.run_turn(interpreter, ...) and practice.workflow.run_turn(FixtureChatModel(...), ...) directly with duck-typed chat objects and assert the call count.
- langgraph P5-07 and agent-framework A08 name the web unit test sse.test.ts; apps/web/package.json runs 'node --experimental-strip-types --test tests/unit/*.test.mjs', so that file would never run (own-loop's sse.test.mjs is right).
- own-loop ('max_turns up to 8 for four-goal scenarios', max_turns carried in the blueprint snapshot): Blueprint.steps has max_length=3 and Blueprint/ConversationBank use extra='forbid' (topic_conversations/schemas.py), so new blueprint fields break an old image during a migration-first release. ConversationBank.unique() also rejects two conversations for one (stage, topic); no proposal mentions relaxing it.
- own-loop and agent-framework add routes under /content-review/scenarios, but routes_content_review.py already has GET and POST /content-review/{stage_id}; unless declared first they are captured as stage_id='scenarios' (langgraph's two-segment path is safe).
- own-loop: 'temperature gives HTTP 400 on every current West Europe model' is overstated. Microsoft's reasoning page (updated 2026-09-29) marks temperature as supported in its GPT-6 table (gpt-6-luna, gpt-6-sol, gpt-6.1-sol, all Data Zone Standard in westeurope) while its text says only GPT-6 Astra accepts it. agent-framework repeats the text, langgraph the table; the source is self-inconsistent, so it must be probed.
- All three omit reasoning.context: Microsoft's stateless guidance is to replay output items and set reasoning.context to all_turns; it is the default only for GPT-5.6 and GPT-6, so langgraph's and agent-framework's gpt-5.4-mini default needs it explicitly.
- Version pin caveat for all three: gpt-5.6-luna/terra/sol are version 2026-07-09 in Microsoft's region and retirement tables but 2026-06-25 in the reasoning page's feature table; confirm with the Models API before pinning.
- agent-framework A05 fails the release on lifecycleStatus 'Deprecating'. Per Microsoft that API value means still serving but closed to new customers, which is the current state of gpt-4.1-mini, so every release before the migration would be blocked; the API value 'Deprecated' is the retired state.
- own-loop A08 relies on closing the client to run on_abort inside the generator. Starlette iterates sync generators in the threadpool and does not close them deterministically on disconnect (not documented either way by FastAPI), and context_dep would keep Depends(get_session) open for the whole stream. Unsafe assumption rather than a proven error; replaced by a runner thread.
- langgraph: the durable-execution resume semantics its checkpointer case rests on could not be verified (the page still does not contain them); the planner said so.
- Bicep detail for own-loop: infra/main.bicep has one chatCapacity and one chatDeploymentSku shared by both deployments (lines 36-38, 247, 253); separate capacities need new parameters.
- Confirmed correct (no error): lock versions (langgraph 1.2.12, langgraph-checkpoint 4.2.0, fastapi 0.141.1, starlette 1.6.0, psycopg 3.3.6, opentelemetry-semantic-conventions 0.65b0); the 14 failure kinds; check_azure_config.py accepts DataZoneStandard; westeurope regional Standard offers only gpt-4.1-mini while Data Zone Standard offers gpt-5.4, gpt-5.4-mini, gpt-5.5, gpt-5.6-*, gpt-6-*; retirement dates 2027-04-14, 2027-09-02, 2027-09-21, 2028-01-11; gpt-5.6 rejects tools with reasoning on Chat Completions and defaults to medium effort; encrypted reasoning items are returned by default when store=false; max_output_tokens covers reasoning tokens; Ollama tool_choice unsupported and /v1/responses since 0.13.3; agent-framework-core 1.20.0 (2026-10-02), chat middleware per model call, ResponseStream gates; langgraph 1.2.13 and langgraph-checkpoint-postgres 3.1.2 (needs psycopg-pool); FastAPI native SSE since 0.135 with sync generators; agent-framework's file:line references.

## Reusable assets

Functions, components and data the roadmap builds on instead of re-inventing.

**Backend platform and delivery**

- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/usage/service.py` — reserve/commit/release/snapshot with SQL-enforced daily+total limits and request-id dedup; UsageLimitExceeded Reuse: Wrap every new LLM/agent/tool step and every streaming call (reserve before the stream, commit measured tokens at the end); expose snapshot() to the UI for an 'allowance left today' indicator.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/jobs/service.py` — Durable Postgres job queue: enqueue (idempotent), claim_next (SKIP LOCKED lease), run_job, drain, JobLoop, JobRegistry Reuse: Base for background agent runs (story/video writers, coach plans, nightly review generation); extend with heartbeat, worker pool and progress events rather than adding a new queue service.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/providers/base.py` — Provider ports: ChatModel (complete, complete_once), SpeechToText, TextToSpeech, BlobStore (incl. get_range), VideoRenderer; ChatResult with token usage Reuse: Extend ChatModel with stream() and tools instead of adding vendor SDK calls in domains; keep fixture parity.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/providers/chat_openai_compatible.py` — OpenAICompatibleChatModel for Ollama and Azure OpenAI v1 (managed identity token provider, retries, semaphore, usage parsing), extract_json_object, schema_instruction Reuse: Add streaming/json_schema/tool-call support here; reuse ChatEndpoint for auth and URLs.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/providers/registry.py` — Composition root build_chat/build_stt/build_tts/build_blob/build_video, Providers dataclass, video_mode Reuse: Single place to add new providers (e.g. an agent runtime or Azure AI Foundry client) and to build per-test provider sets.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/providers/fixtures.py` — FixtureChatModel with keyword rule tables and failure injection (fail_first, fail_calls); FixtureSTT/TTS; MemoryBlobStore; tone_wav Reuse: Deterministic tests and E2E for new agent/tutor flows; add rule entries in tests/fixtures/chat_replies.json keyed by prompt_version.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/practice/workflow.py` — LangGraph TurnState graph: propose_action (model) → validate_action (code) → compose_reply (model, anchored, contradiction check) Reuse: Template for 'model proposes, code decides' agent nodes; compile the graph once instead of per turn; add tool nodes that call deterministic domain functions.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/topic_conversations/workflow.py` — interpret → validate_meaning → compose with complete_once and quote-grounding checks; reservation_tokens estimator Reuse: Pattern for grounded evaluation of learner utterances; reuse validate_meaning-style evidence checks for agent outputs.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/stories/validator.py` — validate_draft, feedback_text, lemma_key, tokens: deterministic level/vocabulary/length critic for generated Dutch text Reuse: Critic step in any generator agent (stories, videos, generated exercises, dialogues) to keep output at the learner's CEFR stage.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/stories/vocab.py and apps/api/src/dlp/domains/stories/today.py` — Word bank with spaced repetition (due_items, save_word, key_for, item_view) and daily points/streak/goal (add_points, streak, DAILY_GOAL, local_today) Reuse: Backend for gamified UI (streaks, daily goal rings, review queues) and as tools/memory for a tutor agent (known words, due words).
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/coaching/service.py` — Practice observations (record_observation, observation_for_request) and deterministic three-activity plan with optional model ranking (complete_once, PlanRanking) Reuse: Seed of a learner model and a 'next best activity' planner agent; expose as a read tool for agents and as the onboarding/'what next' API for the UI.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/api/deps.py` — context_dep (assertion + CSRF + request id + learner), providers_dep, settings_dep Reuse: Reuse for all new endpoints, including SSE; override providers_dep/settings_dep in tests instead of monkeypatching globals.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/api/routes_videos.py` — Range-aware StreamingResponse over BlobStore.get_range (lines 76-121) Reuse: Reference implementation for streaming responses through the existing proxy (which forwards bodies as streams).
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/speech/audio.py and apps/api/src/dlp/domains/speech/service.py` — canonicalise/probe/run_tool hardened ffmpeg wrapper; transcribe_upload/synthesize_text with usage accounting Reuse: Any new speaking/pronunciation feature should go through these for bounds and accounting.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/providers/preflight.py and apps/api/src/dlp/api/routes_health.py` — run_preflight/format_table, Azure 'ok from live evidence' rule, /health, /health/ready (migration head), /health/preflight Reuse: Extend with checks for any new provider/agent runtime; keep readiness tied to the migration head.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/release.py and scripts/azure_release.py` — Advisory-locked migration job with content validation and DML role grants; verify-ci, verify_topology, run_migration, wait_for_revision Reuse: Keep as the release spine; new tables need only Alembic migrations (grants are re-applied on migrate).
- `benchmarks/language/harness.py` — Language-benchmark harness running cases through any ChatModel, tagged by provider/model/prompt_version Reuse: Evaluation harness for new prompts/agents and for choosing Azure model deployments.
- `apps/api/tests/conftest.py` — database fixture (migrations on dlp_test), TRUNCATE list, client fixture, auth_headers() Reuse: Base fixtures for new API/SSE tests; add new tables to TABLES_TO_CLEAR and cli.LEARNER_DATA_TABLES.
- `apps/web/app/api/[...path]/route.ts` — Same-origin proxy: identity strip, CSRF, assertion, forwards upstream.body as a stream Reuse: Already SSE-capable; only add traceparent forwarding and any new response headers to FORWARDED_* lists.
- `scripts/run.py` — Cross-platform tasks (dev, test, e2e with fixture providers and test DB guard) Reuse: Add new tasks (e.g. worker, openapi export, typegen) here rather than shell scripts (D-11).

**Learning domains (API)**

- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/stories/validator.py` — Deterministic level gate validate_draft, coverage/_known stemming, lemma_key, tokens, feedback_text Reuse: Gate any generated exercise/text (cloze passages, dialogues, scenarios) by stage before showing it; feed failures back to the generator for one rewrite.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/stories/bible.py` — PROFILES: StageProfile per stage (word/sentence limits, new words, coverage target, grammar focus, guidance) Reuse: Single source of per-stage difficulty constraints for generators and agents.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/coaching/service.py` — record_observation/observation_for_request (append-only idempotent event log), candidate_plan (pure policy), validate_ranking (model may only reorder), paid_model_guard/_lock Reuse: Template for an activity-event stream and for agent outputs that must be constrained to code-provided candidates.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/curriculum/service.py` — objective_result, assess_productive + validate_productive_reply (grounded rubric with quotes), audio_parts, word_count Reuse: Grade MC sets and open answers; the grounding rule (positive judgement requires exact learner quote) generalises to any model grader.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/curriculum/writing.py` — validate_corrections and Correction categories (spelling, grammar, word_order, punctuation, word_choice) Reuse: Inline-correction UI and a ready error taxonomy for a mistakes table.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/topic_conversations/schemas.py` — normalized() and contains() phrase matcher; Blueprint/Step with required_groups and choices Reuse: Deterministic tolerant matching for typed short answers and role-play goals.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/topic_conversations/workflow.py` — validate_meaning (quote, anchors, choices, negation, injection checks) inside a LangGraph interpret->validate->compose graph Reuse: Pattern for any model-interpreted learner turn; extend the graph with conditional edges for richer tutoring.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/practice/actions.py` — apply_action code-authoritative state machine with AppointmentState Reuse: Template for task-based role-play where the model proposes actions and code commits state.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/feedback/service.py` — Evidence-handle feedback (E1..En), resolve_handles, drop unverifiable points Reuse: Make any agent explanation cite stored evidence; reuse for cross-activity feedback.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/stories/vocab.py` — SM-2 schedule(), save_word, key_for, due_items, stats Reuse: Scheduler for a generalised review-item queue (words, phrases, grammar, mistakes).
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/stories/today.py` — add_points, streak, recent_days Reuse: Move to a shared progress service and call from every domain's completion path.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/stories/service.py` — read_aloud_result token alignment; generate_episode evaluator-optimizer loop; translate_paragraph cached fa translation Reuse: Dictation/shadowing exercises; generic generate-validate-retry helper.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/usage/service.py` — reserve/commit/release with SQL limits Reuse: Budget guard for every agent tool call; wrap in one helper.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/jobs/service.py` — Durable Postgres job queue with registry, idempotency keys, leases Reuse: Run background agent work (profile refresh, exercise generation, weekly review) without new Azure services.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/content_review/service.py` — Admin reviewer agent with path+quote validated issues and versioned cache Reuse: QA pass for generated exercise banks before release.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/providers/registry.py` — Providers(chat, chat_strong, stt, tts, blob, video) with complete/complete_once structured outputs and fixture implementations Reuse: Inject models into agents; fixtures enable deterministic tests.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/speech/service.py` — transcribe_upload / synthesize_text with usage accounting; AudioAsset.meta.transcript Reuse: Speaking/listening exercises and pronunciation-free speaking practice.
- `content/practice/<stage>.json` — 100 topics per stage with title, category, objectives, language_focus, vocabulary_ids, reading/listening texts, speaking/writing tasks (nl/en/fa) Reuse: Seed metadata (situations, categories, objectives, vocab links) for generated, more varied exercises.
- `content/library/<stage>.json` — >=500 words with nl/en/fa meaning and example, >=100 stories per stage Reuse: Source for vocabulary drills, cloze items and auto-enrolled review items.
- `content/curriculum/path.json` — 12 stages with grammar topics (explanation, examples, 1 practice q), core vocabulary, lesson and final check Reuse: Backbone for a guided path and grammar-drill generation.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/coaching/models.py` — practice_observations table (passed, failed_refs, summary, response, content_version) Reuse: Existing error-history data for a learner profile or tutor agent.

**Model layer and agent readiness**

- `apps/api/src/dlp/providers/base.py` — ChatModel (complete / complete_once), ChatMessage, ChatResult, ProviderError / ProviderUnavailable Reuse: Extend with tools, tool_calls and streaming. Keep it as the only seam that agent code calls.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/providers/chat_openai_compatible.py` — ChatEndpoint (Azure v1 URL, managed-identity bearer), extract_json_object, schema_instruction, retry/repair logic Reuse: Add `tools` / `tool_choice` / strict json_schema / stream to the body builder. Reuse the auth and error-sanitising code for a Responses-API adapter.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/providers/registry.py` — build_chat(settings, tier) with small/strong tiers; get_providers / reset_providers Reuse: Add an 'agent' tier or deployment and inject it the same way. Tests swap in fixtures.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/providers/fixtures.py` — FixtureChatModel with per-prompt_version rule tables ('rules' / 'any' / '$last') and failure injection (fail_first / fail_calls) Reuse: Drive deterministic agent-loop tests, including scripted tool-call sequences, keyed by a new prompt_version.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/usage/service.py` — reserve / commit / release with request-id dedup, UsageLimitExceeded, snapshot Reuse: Reserve a per-agent-run budget under a run id and commit per step. Show snapshot() remaining allowance in the UI.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/coaching/service.py` — candidate_plan, validate_ranking, record_observation, history_page, paid_model_guard, _fallback, _reason (localized reasons) Reuse: Use as study-guide agent tools (candidate activities, history) and as the validator pattern for agent-proposed IDs. paid_model_guard serialises paid agent runs per learner.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/stories/vocab.py` — due_items, stats, save_word, review, schedule (SM-2) Reuse: Read-only tools (due words, stats) plus a validated 'save word' tool for a tutor agent. Scheduling stays code-only.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/stories/today.py` — streak, recent_days, add_points, local_today (Europe/Brussels) Reuse: 'Calendar / progress' tool for the study guide. Points stay code-only.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/stories/validator.py` — validate_draft, coverage, tokens, lemma_key, feedback_text, ValidationResult.needs_retry Reuse: Wrap as self-check tools for writers and as the generic 'findings → retry' helper. coverage() can check a tutor's example sentences against the stage level.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/stories/bible.py` — StageProfile PROFILES per stage (word bounds, sentence length, grammar, guidance, coverage) Reuse: Level conditioning for any agent prompt (replace the hard-coded A2).
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/practice/workflow.py` — Anchored generative reply with `_contradicts_decision` guard and fixed-line fallback; LangGraph TurnState pattern Reuse: Template for a generative partner reply in topic conversations and for guarding agent replies against contradicting code decisions.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/topic_conversations/workflow.py` — messages_for, validate_meaning, reservation_tokens, _negated_choice Reuse: Keep as the bounded interpret+validate step. Add a compose-with-model node after it.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/feedback/service.py` — Evidence handles (E1..), describe_evidence, resolve_handles, check_expectations, dropped-point recording Reuse: Citation-grounding pattern for any agent answer about learner work: tool results get handles and uncited claims are dropped.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/curriculum/writing.py` — validate_corrections (exact unique span application) Reuse: The tool a writing-tutor agent must go through to apply edits. Relax it to drop single invalid corrections.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/curriculum/topics.py and curriculum/library.py` — topics_for, topic_page, topic_detail (objectives, language_focus, vocabulary), library_for, vocabulary_page, story_detail Reuse: Read-only curriculum and word-bank lookup tools for tutor and study-guide agents.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/jobs/service.py` — registry.register, enqueue (idempotency key, run_after), JobLoop, drain Reuse: Run longer agent tasks (for example a weekly plan or a batch of content generation) as jobs. Add a lease heartbeat first.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/content_review/service.py` — At-most-once paid job claim (running status committed before the call), text_paths / validate_reply Reuse: Pattern for any background agent run that must never repeat an uncertain paid call. Use as a review gate for agent-generated content.
- `benchmarks/language/harness.py` — Case loader, scoring (exact / any_of / contains_all / json_field), run / summarise, result files tagged with provider/model/prompt/commit Reuse: Base for per-feature and agent eval suites that call the real message builders and validators.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/stories/prompts.py` — BELGIAN_DUTCH register rules constant Reuse: Shared system-prompt fragment for any new agent persona.
- `apps/web/components/PracticeCoach.tsx and apps/web/components/TopicConversation.tsx` — Existing UI entry points for the coach plan and the guided conversation (respond / repeat / hint) Reuse: Places to surface a study-guide agent and a generative partner without new routes in the shell.

**Web app architecture**

- `apps/web/lib/server/identity.ts + lib/server/assertion.ts + lib/server/config.ts` — resolvePrincipal(headers, config), issueAssertion(principal, requestId, config), serverConfig() Reuse: Build lib/server/api.ts for server components/route handlers: `const r = resolvePrincipal(await headers()); fetch(config.apiInternalUrl + path, {headers:{authorization:`Bearer ${await issueAssertion(r.principal, id)}`}})` — enables RSC initial data without the browser round trip.
- `apps/web/app/api/[...path]/route.ts` — Hardened BFF proxy with CSRF, header allow-lists and streaming response Reuse: Keep as the single mutation/audio channel. Extend FORWARDED_RESPONSE_HEADERS and add abort/timeout to support SSE streaming for agent/tutor replies.
- `apps/web/lib/client/api.ts` — apiFetch/apiJson/ApiError/newRequestId + CONNECTIVITY_EVENT Reuse: Use as queryFn/mutationFn inside TanStack Query hooks. Keep the request-id idempotency (pass requestId into mutations).
- `apps/web/lib/client/stories.ts, lib/client/videos.ts` — Typed endpoint-client objects (`stories`, `words`, `videos`) and domain types (TodayPlan with streak/goal/next_step/recent_days, WordItem SRS fields) Reuse: Template for per-domain API modules and query-key factories. TodayPlan already carries the data for a gamified daily-plan home (streak ring, goal bar, day dots).
- `apps/web/lib/client/learning-agents.ts` — PracticePlan (coach/plan suggested|personalised|fallback with reasons) and ConversationSession types; topicHref() safe deep links; practiceStages Reuse: Existing 'coach' recommendation and turn-based conversation contracts to evolve into agent-driven tutoring UIs. topicHref is the validated way to deep-link a recommendation into /learn/[stage]?skill&topic.
- `apps/web/lib/client/phrase-audio.ts + components/PhraseAudio.tsx` — PhraseAudioController (chunked TTS, LRU cache, single speaker, error states) and compact <PhraseAudio text label/> button Reuse: Use as the one 'listen' affordance everywhere (word cards, sentences, options). Restyle only.

- `apps/web/app/stories.css:150-154` — Grade buttons with intent tints (again #fff3ee, hard #fff9ec, good #eef7f1, easy #eaf1fb). Reuse: Model for the semantic danger/warning/success/info subtle backgrounds; promote to tokens.
- `apps/web/app/videos.css:39-43` — Scene list with .scene.is-active (accent border, tinted background, solid accent jump button) and a transition. Reuse: Pattern for the 'current item' state in the path map, the unit sidebar and the read-along highlighting of episode paragraphs.
- `apps/web/app/topics.css:1-3` — .practice-source-switch segmented control driven by aria-pressed. Reuse: Extract as a SegmentedControl/Tabs primitive (also covers the .test-tabs and mobile .unit-sidebar nav patterns).
- `apps/web/app/stories.css:41-45` — writing-dots @keyframes with a reduced-motion fallback. Reuse: Template for new keyframes (pop, shake, pulse, fill, shimmer) with matching reduced-motion overrides.

**Documentation, decisions and constraints**

- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/usage/service.py` — Atomic reserve/commit/release usage counters per metric (model_calls, tokens, audio_seconds, video_seconds) and scope, enforced in SQL Reuse: Reserve before every model or agent step. Multi-step agents should reserve per step with a request-scoped key.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/providers/base.py + chat_openai_compatible.py` — ChatModel.complete (repair plus bounded retries) and complete_once (single attempt, no repair), schema-validated JSON-mode chat completions for Ollama and Azure (v1, managed identity) Reuse: Extend with tool-calling here rather than adding a vendor SDK, which keeps the provider abstraction (ENGINEERING §1).
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/practice/workflow.py, apps/api/src/dlp/domains/topic_conversations/workflow.py` — LangGraph propose/interpret → validate (code) → compose patterns with typed state, fixed-line fallback and a recursion_limit Reuse: Template for any new agent graph. Add tool nodes whose outputs are validated by code.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/stories/validator.py` — Deterministic gate for generated Dutch text (language, length, sentence complexity, vocabulary coverage, evidence-backed questions, gij/ge labelling) with a rewrite-once loop Reuse: Validate generated practice exercises or agent-written content before showing it (D-18 pattern).
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/stories/today.py` — add_points, streak, recent_days, DAILY_GOAL=20 per Europe/Brussels day (learning_days table) Reuse: Hook topic practice, missions, conversations and curriculum completions into points so the daily loop covers practice.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/stories/vocab.py` — SM-2 word bank with REVIEW_POINTS and lapse re-queue after 10 min Reuse: Let the library and topic practice save into it; use it to drive a review step on Today.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/api/routes_stories.py (GET /today)` — Daily-plan aggregator with next_step selection Reuse: Extend next_step and the payload with a practice/conversation step and a coach suggestion.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/coaching/service.py + apps/web/components/PracticeCoach.tsx` — Deterministic three-activity plan with optional single-call model ordering; uses evidence references Reuse: Surface it on the home page; a basis for an 'agentic' tutor that still cannot change the candidates.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/jobs/service.py` — Durable PostgreSQL job queue (leases, backoff, idempotency keys) run by the warm API loop Reuse: Run background agent work or content generation without adding Service Bus or Functions.
- `apps/web/components/LanguageSupport.tsx (LearningText, LanguageSwitcher)` — Dutch plus English/Persian support rendering with RTL Reuse: Use it for bilingual onboarding and orientation copy without dropping Dutch.
- `apps/web/components/ContentLabel.tsx` — Review-status label (Niet-nagekeken inhoud / Content awaiting review) Reuse: Required on any new generated or unreviewed content.
- `apps/web/app/design.css, apps/web/app/stories.css (tone-1..6 gradients), apps/web/components/Icon.tsx` — Current tokens, colourful cover gradients and the icon set Reuse: Starting point for a consolidated, more colourful token system.
- `apps/web/tests/e2e/*.spec.ts (axe scans), design.spec.ts` — Accessibility and CSP regression checks at several widths Reuse: Keep them green while restyling; extend them for new screens.
- `scripts/verify_live.py, scripts/check_azure_config.py, scripts/deploy_current.sh, scripts/azure_release.py, infra/main.bicep` — Release, verification and infrastructure tooling for the live Azure environment Reuse: Ship any new feature through them; add Bicep params for new env vars.
- `docs/VERIFICATION.md status vocabulary; docs/DEMO.md; benchmarks/language/harness.py` — Evidence vocabulary, reviewer walkthrough, 40-case model benchmark Reuse: Record new feature evidence; benchmark any agent or strong-model choice before recording it in DECISIONS.

**Azure agent platform research (web, read 2026-10-05)**

- `apps/api/src/dlp/providers/base.py` — ChatModel ABC with complete/complete_once and ChatResult (tokens, latency, attempts, raw) Reuse: Implement a new AzureResponsesChatModel(ChatModel) that calls /openai/v1/responses with text.format json_schema strict and max_output_tokens. Domain code stays unchanged.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/providers/chat_openai_compatible.py` — ChatEndpoint (URL/auth headers incl. bearer token provider), extract_json_object, schema_instruction, RETRYABLE_STATUS, bounded backoff, semaphore concurrency limit Reuse: Keep for the Ollama path. Reuse the token-provider header logic, error mapping (never forward provider bodies) and semaphore in the Responses adapter. Remove the json_object repair loop on the Azure path.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/providers/registry.py` — build_chat(settings, tier) small/strong selection and _chat_token_provider with lazy DefaultAzureCredential Reuse: Add a settings switch, e.g. AZURE_CHAT_API='responses', to select the new adapter. The small/strong tiering maps directly onto Luna-class and Sol/Terra-class deployments.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/providers/speech_azure.py` — _auth_headers (key or aad#resourceId#token), AzureSpeechToText REST client, parse_recognition, AzureTextToSpeech SSML builder, managed_identity_token_provider Reuse: Add an optional Pronunciation-Assessment header (base64 JSON) and a parse of AccuracyScore/Words[].ErrorType for a scripted nl-NL read-aloud mode. Reuse the SSML builder with nl-BE-ArnaudNeural for a second partner voice.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/usage/service.py` — Atomic reserve/commit/release of per-learner daily and total allowances for model calls, tokens, audio seconds and video seconds Reuse: Wrap every new tutor call (explain-my-mistake, post-conversation feedback, Voice Live session seconds) in reserve→commit/release. An Agent Framework middleware can call it before each chat invocation.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/topic_conversations/workflow.py` — messages_for (untrusted-input system prompt), reservation_tokens, validate_meaning (quote, anchor and negation checks), run_turn interpret→validate→compose Reuse: Keep as the evidence gate for any richer partner. A post-conversation feedback step can reuse the validated quotes as citations.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/observability.py` — Privacy-safe Azure Monitor OTel setup with request spans using only route, method and status Reuse: Add a model-call span helper with GenAI-style low-cardinality attributes (gen_ai.request.model, usage tokens, prompt_version). If adopting Agent Framework, call enable_instrumentation(enable_sensitive_data=False) after configure_azure_monitor.
- `infra/main.bicep` — OpenAI account plus chat-small/chat-strong deployments with chatDeploymentSku allowing DataZoneStandard; Speech S0 with role assignment; budget; App Insights connection string env Reuse: Switch the SKU parameter to DataZoneStandard and set model names and versions. A Voice Live prototype can reuse the Speech resource and identity (Voice Live sits under Azure Speech/Foundry Tools), subject to verification.
- `apps/api/src/dlp/domains/coaching/service.py` — Existing single complete_once call (PlanRanking, 600 tokens, temperature 0) with deterministic fallback and caching Reuse: Template for new bounded tutor calls: deterministic default, one optional model call, cache keyed by history and policy, visible fallback.
